Add an AST-based test that scans production Go files for direct calls to closure-owned mutation primitives (`SetReadSet`, `SetReadRanges`, `SetFileTotalLines`, `AppendAcceptedEvidenceRefs`, `SetLatestProgressDecision`) outside the reducer implementation and documented adapter/computation allowlist.
    2. Keep tests/helpers out of the guard; this is a production architecture tripwire, not a keyword/user-intent heuristic.
    3. Run focused reducer/snapshot tests, then full `go test ./...` and `make`.
- **D1-F8e IngestRound single-owner reducer plan**: audit every read artifact written both by `applyStageOutput` truth-set merge and Mutable setters. Either route it through a typed reducer carrier or document a non-overlapping ownership rule with tests. Goal is to remove N-writer ambiguity without destabilizing stable read scenarios. Status: complete for production closure/handoff writes.
  - Exploration finding: `applyStageOutput` already calls production `ingestEvidenceRound(output.ToolResults)`, and `EvidenceClosure.IngestRound` consumes only `ToolResult` rows to add read coverage, read ranges, file totals, and accepted-evidence refs from `ToolHandoffCarrier`. This is the event-stream owner for tool-derived proof.
  - Residual multi-writer surfaces:
    1. `MutableState.AppendEvidence` appends model/tool-emitted evidence and directly calls `EvidenceClosure.AppendAcceptedEvidenceItems`; this is a stage-output/LLM-evidence owner, not the same source as `ToolResult.Handoff.AcceptedEvidence`.
    2. `MutableState.SetTurnAArtifacts` derives `HandoffCarriers` from TurnA tool/evidence inputs and records source-inventory observation into closure. This is a handoff-snapshot owner and can duplicate accepted-evidence semantics if not represented as a typed reducer input.
    3. `MutableState.SetSourceInventoryAdvisory` / `SetSourceInventoryObservation` update mutable fields and also `RecordSourceInventoryObservation` into closure. This is source-inventory observation ownership, currently coupled to setters.
    4. `EvidenceClosure.SetReadSet` / `SetReadRanges` / `SetFileTotalLines` retain snapshot-replacement semantics for explorer parse output; they must not be replaced with additive `IngestRound` semantics without an explicit stage-snapshot reducer.
    5. `MutableState.MergeFrom` merges forked closure state after parallel/subagent work, so any new reducer must preserve worker-created event folding and not double-ingest parent state.
  - Architecture direction:
    - Rename the current reducer conceptually from "all read ingest" to a typed reducer family with explicit input classes: `ToolResultRound`, `StageEvidenceSnapshot`, `TurnAHandoffSnapshot`, `SourceInventoryObservationSnapshot`, and `ForkClosureDelta`.
    - Keep hard ownership typed by input class, not by stage name, prompt wording, or model rationale. The reducer should be callable from `Mutable` setters so side effects remain centralized, but callers still pass structured carriers.
    - First implementation batch should add a reusable typed reducer entrypoint for `TurnAHandoffSnapshot` and `SourceInventoryObservationSnapshot`, then route the existing setter side effects through it while preserving current behavior.
    - Tests must prove idempotence across repeated `SetTurnAArtifacts`, source-inventory advisory/observation merges, and `applyStageOutput` tool-result ingest; no duplicated accepted evidence, no lost source-class universe, no stale source-inventory observation after clear/reset.
  - Exit criteria:
    - Every closure write in these surfaces has an explicit reducer input-class comment and focused test.
    - No path relies on prompt text, user intent keywords, model prose, elapsed time, or broad heuristic counts to decide reducer routing.
    - Stable read E2E and source-inventory tests continue to pass; reducer changes must not alter read-mode dispatch topology.
  - Final result:
    - Implemented typed reducer input classes and routed tool-result rounds, stage evidence snapshots, TurnA handoff snapshots, source-inventory observation snapshots, and lazy closure init through `EvidenceClosure.IngestEvidenceReducerInput`.
    - Added `read_coverage_delta`, `stage_coverage_snapshot`, and `fork_closure_delta` input classes; explorer pre-read, explorer parse-output coverage, forced-read writeback, ground coverage refresh, pre-complete coverage sync, and mutable fork merge now use the same reducer entrypoint.
    - `AppendEvidence` no longer calls `AppendAcceptedEvidenceItems` directly; source-inventory setters and TurnA handoff no longer call `RecordSourceInventoryObservation` directly; production code no longer calls `SetReadSet` / `SetReadRanges` / `SetFileTotalLines` / `MergeFrom` outside reducer internals.
    - Direct closure mutation remains only inside the reducer internals, lower-level `EvidenceClosure` APIs, and tests. This preserves the lower-level API for unit tests while giving production a single typed reducer entrypoint.
    - Validation passed: `go test ./internal/types ./internal/tool ./internal/agent ./internal/orchestrator -run 'EvidenceRound|AcceptedEvidence|TurnA|SourceInventory|ReadCoverage|ApplyStageOutput|EvidenceClosure|Handoff'`, `go test ./internal/types ./internal/orchestrator`, `go test ./internal/agent ./internal/orchestrator ./internal/tool ./internal/tool/ground -run 'EvidenceRound|ReadCoverage|ForcedRead|Coverage|SourceInventory|InvestigationComplete|Explorer'`, `go test ./...`, and `make`.

验证：
- Focused tests for duplicate carrier collapse, rich-row coverage, analyzer required-file demotion, forced-read pruning, and navigation render noise boundaries.
- `go test ./...` and `make`.
- Eval batch: at minimum rerun ArkTS source-inventory, Cangjie repo-wide, Cangjie fixture, plus three non-source-inventory read-mode stability cases to prove the cutover does not regress routine read answers.

退出标准：
- Functionally correct source-inventory answers have exactly one visible carrier per principal row unless the user explicitly requested multiple views.
- `contract_warning` is absent when every requested principal member row is present with typed citations/dimensions.
- Repo-wide source-inventory evals use earlier `repo_map(view="source_inventory")`, fewer forced support reads, lower context usage, and no repeated "验证还不够稳" loop.
- Any remaining failure becomes a named gap in this ledger before the next code batch starts; newly discovered implementation tasks must be added to this task list before code changes begin.

### Batch D1-F9: Staged Execution Kernel Cutover Follow-ups

目标：
- 将外部 prompt 里仍成立的 staged-execution gap 转成可执行 cutover 批次，优先修真正影响架构稳定性的控制面 gap。
- 保持现有稳定 stage shell、BaseAgent ReAct loop、controller-first write workflow、typed criteria 和 EvidenceClosure reducer family；不引入大 GraphExecutor，不用 prompt/用户关键词/模型散文作 hard route。

任务顺序：
1. **D1-F9a hard-dependency readiness authority**：为 TaskGraph 建 typed predecessor-readiness projection，先加测试证明当前 explore window 对 hard_dependency 的行为，再让 ready-window 合并 `NodeExecStatus`、hard_dependency、`EntryConditions` 三类 precise signal。Optional/soft edges 保持非 hard block。Status: complete.
   - Original code fact at `f21a56e0a`/`1d64b1f07`: `scheduler.go` header said hard-dependency predecessors must be `nodeDone`, but `readyExplorerWindowContext` checked only node status plus `EntryConditions`; `TestGraphState_ReadyWindow_MergedInitial` explicitly expected the old merged chain window.
   - Implementation tasks:
     1. Add a typed helper such as `hardDependencyBlockersForNode` / `nodeHardDependenciesSatisfied` that scans only `TaskGraph.Edges` where `EdgeType == EdgeHardDependency` and `To == nodeID`; unknown predecessor ids remain a gate/analyzer validation issue and should not panic the scheduler.
     2. Update `readyExplorerWindowContext` to skip non-optional nodes whose hard predecessors are not `nodeDone`, and keep optional nodes silent when their predecessors are not ready so routine status does not spam blocked optional nodes.
     3. Extend `nodeBlock` with dependency blocker ids or add a sibling typed blocker carrier so forced-finalize / render hints can distinguish entry-condition failures from dependency-wait state without parsing prose.
     4. Update scheduler unit tests from "merged initial chain" to "probe first, evidence after probe done, validate after evidence done"; add closure-status coverage so predecessor checks consume `EvidenceClosure.NodeExecStatus` over stale bootstrap maps.
     5. Update dispatch split tests that assumed all hard-dependent siblings were in the first window; preserve independent sibling parallelism only when their shared predecessor is already done.
     6. Run focused scheduler/dag tests before any broader read tests: `go test ./internal/orchestrator -run 'GraphState|ReadyWindow|Dependency|DagNode|AnalyzeRefine|SourceInventory|ExtractInputReady|IRDeliveryHotFileLineRatchet'`.
   - Delivered:
     1. `scheduler_dependency.go` centralizes hard-dependency blocker extraction and typed blocker rendering. `readyExplorerWindowContext` now gates pending/requeued explore-family nodes on `EdgeHardDependency` predecessors before evaluating entry conditions.
     2. Routine dependency waits stay silent while another ready node exists; blocker text only appears when the scheduler has no ready window, reducing repeated user-visible "waiting" noise.
     3. `scheduler_hard_stall.go` preserves hard-stall handoff by injecting inconclusive hypothesis verdicts before forced finalize.
     4. `stage_output_evidence_ingest.go` closes D1-G62 by routing StageOutput evidence into the same Mutable proof/reducer view used by CGEC, handoff, and finalizer paths.
     5. Checkpoint evidence counts now dedupe BusContext/output/Mutable/TurnA evidence views instead of summing parallel carriers.
   - Validation:
     - `go test ./internal/orchestrator -run 'TestRunTaskGraph_ParallelDispatch_CanBeForcedSerial|TestApplyStageOutput_IngestsEvidenceItemsIntoMutableOnce|TestBuildExploreTransientRetryCheckpointHintDedupsEvidenceViews|TestRunTaskGraph_RetryableExploreErrorAfterEvidenceContinuesFromCheckpoint' -count=1 -v`
     - `go test ./internal/orchestrator`
     - `go test ./internal/types ./internal/analysis/compiler ./internal/analysis/criterion ./internal/agent -run 'Evidence|Dependency|Artifact|ReadLoopNextAction|DispatchPolicy|ExecutionPolicy|StageRunner|EvidenceReducer|ReadRunSnapshot'`
     - `go test ./...`
     - `make`
   - Remaining follow-up:
     - D1-F9a.2 / D1-G63 turns the predecessor gate into a single `NodeReadiness` authority before more scheduler cutover. The first batch defines a typed readiness result with `ready/blocked/skip/reason_code/waiting_on/failed_criteria`, centralizes failed predecessor semantics, keeps optional dependency waits silent, preserves cancellation checks, and adds tests for failed predecessor, validation-feedback requeue, cancellation, optional silence, closure-status precedence, and current read E2E.
     - This is not a prompt change and must not inspect user text, model rationale, retry hint prose, elapsed time, or fuzzy counts.
   - D1-F9a.2 detailed task list:
     1. Add an unexported scheduler-local `nodeReadiness` typed view plus closed reason-code constants in a new concern file. It must carry `NodeID`, normalized status, `Ready`, `Blocked`, `Skip`, `ReasonCode`, `WaitingOn`, `FailedPredecessors`, and `FailedCriteria`.
     2. Replace the duplicated checks inside `readyExplorerWindowContext` with `evaluateNodeReadinessContext(ctx,node,env)`. The ready-window renderer should convert only `Blocked` readiness into `nodeBlock`; skipped optional/status/stage nodes must stay silent.
     3. Split hard-dependency waiting from failed predecessor semantics. Pending/running/requeued predecessors stay `waiting_on_dependency`; failed predecessors become `failed_dependency` so future recovery/replay can decide skip/block without parsing "waiting for" prose.
     4. Keep current commercial behavior for the first cutover: failed hard predecessor still blocks downstream from dispatching and surfaces only when no ready node exists. The change is authority consolidation and typed reason surfacing, not a new recovery policy.
     5. Add focused tests for: hard predecessor pending, hard predecessor failed, optional dependency wait silence, optional entry-condition silence, closure status wins over stale bootstrap state, validation-feedback requeue readiness, cancellation propagation, and current read E2E no deadlock.
     6. Update this ledger after implementation with the exact validation commands. If this reveals broader MaxRetries/OneShot policy gaps that cannot fit safely, record them as D1-G63.x before coding another batch rather than lowering the standard.

Focused tests cover typed authority, closure status precedence, cancellation propagation, failed hard predecessor, optional dependency silence, optional entry-condition silence, validation-feedback requeue, extract/finalize exclusion, AnalyzeRefine optional silence/true path, and current hard-dependency read E2E.

Clone/merge/reset must preserve or clear it with the same lifecycle as node status; fork merge must keep the maximum observed attempt per node, not append duplicate events.

     4. Move attempt increment to the single actual dispatch entrypoint: `graphState.markRunning`. Attempts count dispatch starts. `MaxRetries <= 0` means no node-local cap. For `MaxRetries > 0`, allowed dispatches are `1 + MaxRetries`; readiness blocks only a `nodeRequeued` node whose consumed attempts already reached that limit, so first pending dispatch is never blocked.
     5. Add `max_retries_exhausted` as a closed `nodeReadinessReason` and carry `AttemptsUsed` / `MaxAttempts` into `nodeReadiness` and `nodeBlock` for future status cards/replay. This is a typed hard gate over IR declaration + closure counter only; no retry hint prose, model rationale, elapsed-time heuristic, or user keyword may influence it.
     6. Preserve existing graph-level `ExecutionPolicy.RetryBudget`, transient retry budget, validation-feedback budget, and finalizer-local retry semantics. D1-F9a.3 only prevents a requeued node from being dispatched beyond its own declared cap; it does not reinterpret unrelated budgets.
     7. Add focused tests for: initial pending dispatch allowed, first requeue within cap allowed, exhausted requeue blocked with typed reason, `MaxRetries=0` unlimited, `markRunning` increments once per dispatch, closure/snapshot/reducer roundtrip, bad snapshot node attempts rejected, validation-feedback requeue respects node-local cap, and current read E2E no deadlock.

It must not by itself reopen broad code exploration after an accepted/stable investigation closure.
     3. Implemented local normalization for exact `member_set` count metadata: `members[]` is the authoritative typed boundary, so non-integer/negative/drifted `value` surfaces are canonicalized to `len(members)` instead of forcing another model retry.
     4. Implemented local decorated-member form repair before the support-ref validator: ordinary resolved current-source members shaped like `CodeSymbol (qualifier)` are normalized to bare member identity plus aligned `member_notes`, with closed provenance `form_repair:decorated_member_base`. This preserves the answer content while avoiding downstream citation alignment failure and avoids asking the model to manually remove presentation qualifiers. The repair explicitly excludes absence lanes, VCS/runtime/command/external origin-specific lanes, and diagnostic lanes that require current-source verification; those still require explicit support refs or the existing origin-specific carrier.
     5. Added `RepairOriginCompletionFormPrefix` and classified framework-owned completion-form `RepairStructuredHandoff` as advisory/non-blocking for accepted closure. This keeps future completion-form repair descriptors from becoming new broad exploration blockers.
     6. What remains hard: illegal enums, mutually exclusive control fields, precise typed contradictions, and exact principal-member omissions that can change the answer set. What must stay soft/advisory: display-only citation carrier issues, visible prose/table count parsing, incomplete navigation lenses that are not the answer lane, malformed optional waiver payloads after D1-F10g.44, and completion-form/schema-adjacent handoff defects after evidence closure.

It does not inspect raw user wording, prompt text, model rationale, final-answer prose, repo_map rendered markdown, grep counts, wall time, or REPL status text as control logic.

The model read only visible fixture `.cj` rows and closed with packages `demo.bridge/demo.cart/demo.app`, while the typed source-class census had same-language thirdparty Cangjie sample scopes that were still unchecked; final answer missed `demo.stringext/demo.ffi/demo.greeter`.
     2. Root cause: `sourceInventoryResolvedCompletionDebtIsAdvisory` treated all source-inventory completion debt as advisory, including `FollowupDebt{reason_code=missing_source_class_family}` with concrete same-language sample scopes. This is too weak for explicit inventory tasks: precise, executable missing-source-class debt must drive one bounded follow-up before completion, while non-precise pagination/candidate-budget debt remains caveat-only.
     3. Landing fix: hard gates stay limited to typed precision. If the completion authority carries active `missing_source_class_family`, non-empty follow-up scopes, missing classes, covered classes, and covered language family, `emit_investigation_complete` downgrades with a narrow `repo_map(view=source_inventory)` follow-up and renders the typed path/scopes/query/cursor in the tool result. Pure wide pagination, root table truncation, or no-sample source-class debt continues to append a typed caveat and complete after the model-owned answer boundary is otherwise closed.
     4. Loop safety: this reuses the existing low-delta downgrade convergence boundary; repeated identical source-inventory blockers converge with a typed caveat instead of reopening broad exploration indefinitely.

Analyzer/tool consistency fix: typed source-inventory requests cannot simultaneously allow repo-owned auxiliary classes as principal members and exclude fixture/example/test/generated classes. `emit_analysis` now rejects that typed contradiction instead of letting downstream `list_files` silently hide auxiliary `.cj/.ets` files; list_files has Cangjie-specific coverage proving typed source-inventory root discovery auto-includes repo-owned fixture/thirdparty Cangjie while still excluding dependency/cache/eval-result noise.

Red-line boundary: consumes only `SourceInventoryCompletionAuthority`, `SourceInventoryFollowupDebt`, typed source-class/language census, roles, sample scopes, `SourceScopeProfile`, and typed `AnswerExclusionPolicy` enums.   - **Delivered D1-F10g.132 REPL second-turn classifier hard-timeout and stale-dock recovery（code complete / focused regression passed）**:
    1. Target gap: REPL 第二次提问可卡在“请求模型中”，总计时器不刷新且没有后续 pipeline 日志。根因边界是 turn-policy/chitchat classifier 位于正式 orchestrator pipeline 之前；如果 provider adapter 不响应 context deadline，主 goroutine 会停在分类 LLM call 里，后续日志和阶段状态都不会出现。
    2. Runtime change: structured `ClassifyPolicy` 与 legacy `Classify` 统一通过 `chatWithClassifierHardTimeout` 调用 adapter；即使 adapter 忽略 context，也会在 `turnPolicyClassifierTimeout` 到期后返回 `context.DeadlineExceeded`，让 REPL fail-safe 落回 repo pipeline。
    3. Fallback change: structured turn-policy 超时后不再立即调用同一个 legacy chitchat classifier provider，避免把一次卡顿扩展成第二次卡顿；其它非超时分类错误仍保留既有 fallback 语义。
    4. UI lifecycle change: renderer 开始新 spinner 时如果发现 live dock 指针仍在但 animation ticker 已停，会清理半关闭 dock 并启动新 lifecycle，避免第二轮状态栏停留在旧的静态“请求模型中”行。
    5. Red-line boundary: 本批只消费 context deadline、adapter lifecycle、renderer dock/ticker 状态和现有 classifier timeout 配置。它不读取用户词面、模型 rationale、prompt prose、工具 summary、localized status 文案、final answer prose、elapsed-time 文案或 eval label 来决定路由。

  - **Delivered D1-F10g.137 source-inventory principal row-set source-family contamination boundary（code complete / focused regression passed）**:
    1. Target gap: D2-F10g136 representative eval was 6/6 PASS, but manual audit of `arkts_repomap` found the finalizer was forced to append Go helper/test rows (`extractStableBuilderIdentity`, `Test*Builder*`) under `source inventory principal rows`. These rows were navigation/support facts for the Go implementation of ArkTS extraction, not principal ArkTS `@Entry` / `@Builder` declarations.
    2. Root cause: the synthetic source-inventory principal row-set projection filtered by requested role and completion state, but when a grounded model member_set already existed, it did not narrow system-authored supplement rows to the same source family. A same-name construct family such as "Builder" therefore allowed different language/test/helper rows to become hard answer obligations.
    3. If normalization fails and aggregate facts are optional for the current result kind, it already drops invalid individual facts and keeps valid siblings. Existing tests cover this optional-drop path for runtime aggregates, while `NormalizeAnswerAggregateFacts` correctly rejects `negative_observation` without a non-repo origin. The gap is that current-source contextual negatives can still be treated as non-optional in ordinary resolved completions, producing a model-visible retry even when principal member_set facts are already valid.
